[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: DRAGONFLY
Tooltip: Garden Wisdom I Grants +0.5 ☯ Farming Wisdom.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 15.; EPIC FOREST SHARD (ID E37)
The Dragonfly Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Garden Wisdom]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Dragonfly Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Dragonfly]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Dragonfly Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Firefly Shard]]
- Second: Dragon Family
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Dragonfly Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Garden Wisdom]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Garden Wisdom ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Farming Wisdom]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Dragonfly Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Dragonfly Shard has the following description:
- Dragonflies soar through the air with the grace and power of their mythical namesakes.

# Dusty Travel Scroll to the Kuudra Skull
Aliases: Travel Scroll to Magma Fields
Categories: Epic items, Items, More info needed, Travel Scrolls
[Dusty Travel Scroll to the Kuudra Skull]
Type: Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NETHER_FORTRESS_BOSS_TRAVEL_SCROLL
Tooltip: Consume this item to add its destination to your Fast Travel options.; Requires MVP+ to consume!; Island: Crimson Isle Teleport: Forgotten Skull; EPIC TRAVEL SCROLL
The Dusty Travel Scroll to the Kuudra Skull is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Travel Scrolls|Travel Scroll]] that unlocks [[Fast Travel]] to the [[Forgotten Skull]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Drops
The Dusty Travel Scroll to the Kuudra Skull has a [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) chance to be obtained as a [[Kuudra|reward]] from the [[Kuudra]] fight.
## Usage
### Fast Travel
The Dusty Travel Scroll to the Kuudra Skull can be consumed if the player has 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] or higher to unlock the [[Fast Travel]] option to the [[Forgotten Skull]] on the [[Crimson Isle]]. This includes access to /warp kuudra and /warp skull.
### Portalizer
The Dusty Travel Scroll to the Kuudra Skull can be combined with a [[Portalizer]] in an [[Anvil]] to create the [[Portals|Portal to the Forgotten Skull]].

# Dwarf Turtle Shelmet
Aliases: Shelmet
Categories: Epic items, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Pet items, Mythological Ritual
[Dwarf Turtle Shelmet]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Hephaestus Shelmet]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 250,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DWARF_TURTLE_SHELMET
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Grants +10 True Defense and makes the pet's owner immune to knockback.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; EPIC PET ITEM
A Dwarf Turtle Shelmet is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that grants [[Knockback]] immunity and 10 [[True Defense]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv240]|Empyrean Gaia Construct ([[Gaia Construct]])|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.6%|
|[Lv203]|Exalted Gaia Construct ([[Gaia Construct]])|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|
|[Lv164]|Venerable Gaia Construct ([[Gaia Construct]])|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.4%|
|[Lv106]|Stalwart Gaia Construct ([[Gaia Construct]])|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.35%|
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Dwarf Turtle Shelmet will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it [[Knockback]] immunity and +10 [[True Defense]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Dwarf Turtle Shelmet for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hephaestus Shelmet]]|None|1x [[Hephaestus Anvil]] [[Dwarf Turtle Shelmet]] 3x Dwarf Turtle Shelmet|Recipe: Dwarf Turtle Shelmet ×3 + [[Hephaestus Anvil]] → [[Hephaestus Shelmet]]|
## Tips
- In most circumstances, a Dwarf Turtle Shelmet is unnecessary as the [[Attributes|Shell]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] does not need to be equipped to a [[Pets|Pet]]; in addition, playing as the [[Tank]] class in [[Dungeons]] also gives [[Knockback]] immunity. However, its +10 [[True Defense]] can prove useful in circumstances.
  - Using the [[Slime Hat]] is also an alternative, although it replaces the [[Helmets|Helmet]].
## Trivia
- The Dwarf Turtle Shelmet used to be the only [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] in the game to not give any stat bonuses.
- Although it is not normally possible to obtain a Dwarf Turtle Shelmet inside the [[Rift Dimension]], if a player manages to do so anyway and apply it to their [[Montezuma Pet]], it will actually grant them immunity to knockback. However, the pet item gets wiped once the player leaves the Rift.
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]

The Dwarven Geode is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Auspicious]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] or the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Fortune]].
## Obtaining
The Dwarven Geode can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain III (3)]]. It requires 128 (2 stacks) [[Enchanted Cobblestone]], 64 (1 stack) [[Treasurite]], and 6 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Dwarven Geode]] 1 Dwarven Geode
  - 128 [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] 20,480 (320 stacks) [[Cobblestone]]
  - 64 [[Treasurite]]
## Usage
### Reforging
Dwarven Geode can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Auspicious]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Mining Speed]], which scale with the Mining Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXI (21)]]. A Mining Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Auspicious]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Auspicious Bonus, which grants +1% [[Mining Fortune]].
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+3 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +10 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+6 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +20 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+9 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +30 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+13 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +40 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+16 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +50 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+20 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +75 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+25 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +100 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Auspicious Bonus; Grants +1% Mining Fortune.|Auspicious Bonus; Grants +1% Mining Fortune.|Auspicious Bonus; Grants +1% Mining Fortune.|Auspicious Bonus; Grants +1% Mining Fortune.|Auspicious Bonus; Grants +1% Mining Fortune.|Auspicious Bonus; Grants +1% Mining Fortune.|Auspicious Bonus; Grants +1% Mining Fortune.|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|

Dwarven Handwarmers are [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Gloves]] that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].

# Dwarven O's Gemstone Grahams
Categories: Epic items, Items
[Dwarven O's Gemstone Grahams]
Type: Consumable Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: The first 5 times you consume this, gain +2 [[Gemstone Fortune]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DWARVEN_OS_GEMSTONE_GRAHAMS
Tooltip: Consumable; The first 5 times you consume this, gain a permanent +2 Gemstone Fortune.; Right-click to consume!; EPIC
Dwarven O's Gemstone Grahams are an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Consumables|Consumable]]. They are obtained by completing [[Crystal Nucleus]] runs.
## Obtaining
Dwarven O's Gemstone Grahams has a 0.4719868% chance to drop from the [[Crystal Nucleus]] upon completing a Nucleus Run, and has a weight of 1,450.
## Usage
Dwarven O's Gemstone Grahams can be consumed up to 5 times. Each Dwarven O's Gemstone Grahams eaten permanently increases the player's [[Gemstone Fortune]] by +2 , up to +10 [[Gemstone Fortune]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Random Message|
|-|-|
|Less than 5 eaten|Mmm, rock and stone!|
|Less than 5 eaten|Mmm, for Karl!|
|Less than 5 eaten|Mmm, they're magically delicious!|
|Less than 5 eaten|Mmm, they're grrreat!|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|Haven't you already had enough?|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|You can't seem to get the packaging off this one...|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|I don't think I should go over my calorie limit for today.|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|I think you should leave some for the rest of the players.|
## Trivia
- The messages for eating Dwarven O's Gemstone Grahams are each references.
  - Both "rock and stone" and "for Karl!" reference Deep Rock Galactic.
  - "They're magically delicious!" is the tagline for Lucky Charms.
  - "They're grrreat!!" is the tagline for Frosted Flakes.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 20, 2024|Added Dwarven O's Gemstone Grahams.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Added Dwarven O's Gemstone Grahams.|

Categories: Epic items, Items, Decoration
[Dwarven Tankard]
Type: Decoration item

Source: [[Dwarven Tavern]]
[Item Properties]

Sell: 10 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DWARVEN_TANKARD
Tooltip: Decoration Item; The most stylish Dwarven drinking implement built to hold as much ale as possible.; * Co-op Soulbound * EPIC
The Dwarven Tankard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Decoration item, obtainable from [[Hornum]].
## Obtaining
To obtain the Dwarven Tankard, talk to one of the NPCs in the [[Dwarven Tavern]] and then type "burp" in the public chat (note: party chat, guild chat, and private chat will not work) - this will only be visible in chat to players who are also in the [[Dwarven Tavern]] area. A player can only receive one Dwarven Tankard.
Once obtained, you can repurchase the Dwarven Tankard from [[Rusty]] for 100,000 [[Coins]].

This blade becomes stronger as you carry more coins in your purse (Capped at 2B coins); Receive Curse of Greed when striking, CANCELLING any effect modifying your coins loss on death!; Current Damage Bonus: 0.0; This item can be reforged!; EPIC SWORD
Emerald Blade is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]] that becomes stronger as the more [[Coins]] the player carries in their purse (caps at 2B [[Coins]]).
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Emerald VIII (8) ([[Emerald]])|1x [[Stick]] 2x [[Enchanted Emerald Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Emerald Block]] ×2 + [[Stick]] → Emerald Blade|
## Usage
Sword:
Emerald Blade [[Damage]]: +130
[Effects]
[A powerful blade made from pure Emeralds (Emerald). This blade becomes stronger as you carry more Coins in your purse (Capped at 2B coins) Receive Curse of Greed when striking, CANCELLING any effect modifying your Coins loss on death! Current Damage Bonus: 0.0]
Emerald Blade gains bonus [[Damage]] the more coins the wielder carries in their Purse.
### Damage based on coins
DMG=130+2.5×Coins1/4
Although the damage in the description is rounded to 1 decimal place, it will be rounded to the nearest integer when it actually hits the mob.
The damage bonus from [[One For All]] works on the bonus damage.
#### Calculator
calcpage   = Calculator:Emerald Blade damage
 template  = Template:Calculator/Emerald Blade damage
 form      = emBladeDmgCalcForm
 result    = inner

 param     = coins|Coins|1,000,000|string
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
### Coins based on damage
Coins=(0.4×DMG−52)4

|-|-|-|
|0 [[Coins]]|130 (+0)||
|100 [[Coins]]|138 (+8)||
|1,000 [[Coins]]|144 (+14)||
|4,096 [[Coins]]|150 (+20)|[[Leaping Sword]]'s [[Damage]]; 100k [[Coins]] [[Midas' Sword]]'s [[Damage]]|
|10,000 [[Coins]]|155 (+25)|[[Voidedge Katana]]'s [[Damage]]|
|100k [[Coins]]|174 (+44)||
|614.7k [[Coins]]|200 (+70)|[[Pigman Sword]]'s [[Damage]]|
|21.4M [[Coins]]|300 (+170)|[[Shadow Fury]]'s [[Damage]]|
|26.9M [[Coins]]|310 (+180)|Fragged [[Shadow Fury]]'s [[Damage]]|
|43.5M [[Coins]]|333 (+203)|[[Reaper Scythe]]'s [[Damage]]|
|100M [[Coins]]|380 (+250)||
|480M [[Coins]]|500 (+370)|[[Giant's Sword]]'s [[Damage]]; [[Dark Claymore]]'s [[Damage]]|
|1B [[Coins]]|574 (+444‬)||

|14.7B [[Coins]]|1,000 (+870)||
|25.6B [[Coins]]|1,129.9 (+999.9)|Former maximum damage bonus cap|
|1T [[Coins]]|2,630 (+2,500)||
|263 - 1 coins; 9.2Qi [[Coins]]|137,902 (+137,772)|Most likely the limit of coins that could be held in the purse|
|2.560 × 1034 coins; 25.6De [[Coins]]|1,000,000,000 (+999,999,870)||
|21024 - 1 coins|2.89 × 1077|Highest number that can be stored within 64 bits (IEEE-754 floating point)|
### Curse of Greed
When attacking with an Emerald Blade, the player receives Curse of Greed, which cancels all abilities that reduce Coin loss on death.
### [[Romero and Juliette]]
An Emerald Blade is required to complete step 8 of the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest.
## Trivia
- The maximum damage bonus an Emerald Blade could safely have before being capped was 999.9, which took 25,594,000,000 [[Coins]] to achieve.
  - It was possible to surpass this bonus, but it resulted in glitchy behavior such as immobilizing the player and showing shop GUIs wrongly. Upon switching lobbies, the Emerald Blades were deleted. This was discovered by the YouTuber Dragoon.
- Players who suffered from a bug upon the addition of the Curse of Greed effect had their [[Coins]] refunded.
- Drinking a [[Milk Bucket]] or [[Magical Milk Bucket]] will remove the Curse of Greed effect.
  - It's worth noting that this will also get rid of all of your other active potion effects, including ones from the [[God Potion]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 22, 2022|Maximum damage bonus cap has been added at 2B [[Coins]].|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Emerald Blade as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Emerald Blade.|
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Damage to coins in purse scale has been changed.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Maximum damage bonus cap has been added at 2B [[Coins]].|
|Sep 28, 2022|0.15|Now gives Curse of Greed when attacking.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Emerald Blade as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

The Empty Thunder Bottle is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Glass Bottle]] 4x [[Orb of Energy]]|Recipe: [[Orb of Energy]] ×4 + [[Glass Bottle]] → Empty Thunder Bottle|
## Usage
The player can charge an Empty Thunder Bottle by being struck by lightning and through other methods. Once completely charged up (at 50,000 Charges), it becomes a Thunder in a Bottle.
Much like the [[Expertise]] enchantment, only one Bottle will have charge added to it at a time when multiple are in a player's inventory, starting with the leftmost hotbar slot.

Categories: Epic items, Minion fuels, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Coal Collection, Minion Modifiers, Enchanted

Next →: [[Magma Bucket]]
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTED_LAVA_BUCKET
Tooltip: Minion Fuel; Increases the speed of your minion by 25%. Unlimited Duration!; EPIC
The Enchanted Lava Bucket is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Minion Fuel]] that increases [[Minions|Minion]] speed by 25% for unlimited duration. Same as every fuel with unlimited duration, they can be taken from [[Minions]] without being consumed.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Coal VIII (8) ([[Coal]])|2x [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 3x [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] ×3 + [[Enchanted Coal Block]] x2 → Enchanted Lava Bucket|
## Usage
### Minion Fuel
Enchanted Lava Buckets increase Minion speed by 25% and have an unlimited duration. Unlike consumable fuels they can be taken from [[Minions]] to be reused. Enchanted Lava Bucket can be placed in all [[Minions]].
### Quest
An Enchanted Lava Bucket is required on step 6 of the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest, where [[Romero]] is found in the [[Gold Mine]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Magma Bucket]]|None|1x [[Heat Core]] [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]] 2x Enchanted Lava Bucket|Recipe: Enchanted Lava Bucket ×2 + [[Heat Core]] → [[Magma Bucket]]|
## Trivia
- Prior to version 0.9.5, Enchanted Lava Buckets could be emptied by placing their lava. This was not recommended as it would turn them into regular lava buckets.
## History

|Oct 2, 2020|0.9.5|Fixed a bug that let Enchanted Lava Buckets place lava.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed the [[Bazaar]] treating Enchanted Lava Buckets as stackable items.|

# End Stone Bow
Aliases: Endstone Bow, Endstone bow
Categories: Epic items, Weapons, Mob Drops, Bows, The End, Items
[End Stone Bow]
Type: Bow ([[Bows]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Combat Level ([[Combat]]) Requirement: Combat XV (15) ([[Combat]])
Stats: [[Damage]]: +140
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Sell: 2,500 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: END_STONE_BOW
Tooltip: Damage: +140; Ability: Extreme Focus LEFT CLICK Consumes all your mana, and your next hit will deal that much more damage!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 15.; EPIC BOW
The End Stone Bow is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]] that requires [[Combat|Combat XV (15)]] to use. It is a pretty good early-game bow and it can be used as a weaker alternative for the [[Runaan's Bow]].
The ability is a direct parallel to the [[End Stone Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv55]|[[Seer]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.1%|
## Usage
Bow:
End Stone Bow [[Damage]]: +140
[Effects]
[Ability: Extreme Focus LEFT CLICK; Consumes all your Mana, and your next hit will deal that much more Damage!; Mana Cost (Intelligence): All]
Note: Extreme Focus is only applied after all other damage boosters are applied. It does not trigger if a shot is missed. It also only applies to the End Stone Bow and cannot be stacked on other bows. It can also be [[Salvaging|salvaged]] for 3 [[Dragon Essence]].
## Trivia
- The mana points are taken when the ability is used, not when the next arrow connects, making it possible to double fire the extreme focus by letting your mana charge, then using it again after your first shot connects.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: END_STONE_SWORD
Tooltip: Damage: +120; Strength: +80; Ability: Extreme Focus RIGHT CLICK While in the The End, consume all of your remaining  Mana and gain the following buffs.; for every 100 Mana consumed:; Damage Reduction: 1% (last 5s); Next Hit Damage: +25%; Capped at 20% Damage Reduction and +500%; Damage.; Mana Cost: 20; This item can be reforged!; EPIC SWORD
The End Stone Sword is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]] unlocked at [[End Stone|End Stone X (10)]]. Its ability drains its user of all Mana and grants damage reduction and bonus damage.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|End Stone X (10) ([[End Stone]])|1x [[Stick]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted End Stone]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted End Stone]] x64 ×2 + [[Stick]] → End Stone Sword|
## Usage
Sword:
End Stone Sword [[Damage]]: +120 [[Strength]]: +80
[Effects]
[Ability: Extreme Focus RIGHT CLICK; While in The End, consume all of your remaining Mana and gain the following buffs for every 100 Mana consumed: Damage Reduction: 1% (Max 20%.Last 5s) Next Hit Damage: +25% (Max 500%); Mana Cost (Intelligence): 20]
The End Stone Sword can be used as a melee weapon.
When its ability is used, it consumes all of its user's Mana. For every 100 [[Mana]] consumed, it gives +1% damage resistance for 5 seconds up to 20%, and a subsequent hit within 5 seconds will have its damage boosted by 25% up to 500% and can only be used in [[The End]]
The End Stone Sword's damage resistance does not apply to fall damage.
If the ability is activated again while still active, the new damage resistance and damage boost will override the original.
## Trivia
- The End Stone Sword's item ability is similar to that of the [[End Stone Bow]]'s, which consumes all [[Mana]] and does that much more damage on the next shot.
- The End Stone Sword ability was used to effectively skip the Terracotta phase in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]. As the ability no longer works in Dungeons, this no longer works.
- The ability of this sword was used to fight the [[Voidgloom Seraph]], especially in higher tiers. This was due to the fact that when this ability consumes 500 [[Mana]], it allowed the user to be completely immune to most forms of damage for a short period of time.

Tooltip: Health: +20; Defense: +35; All stats on this armor piece are multiplied by 2x while on the End Island!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 12.; * Co-op Soulbound * EPIC HELMET
Ender Armor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] and [[Equipment]] set. It requires [[Combat|Combat XII (12)]] to use.
## Obtaining
Ender Armor has 4 [[Armor]] pieces and 4 [[Equipment]] pieces, to make it an 8 piece set. These are obtained in various ways:
|Slot|Name|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|
|[[Ender Armor]]|Ender Helmet|Has a 1% chance to drop from an [[Enderman]].|
|[[Ender Armor]]|Ender Chestplate|(same as above)|
|[[Ender Armor]]|Ender Leggings|(same as above)|
|[[Ender Armor]]|Ender Boots|(same as above)|
|[[Ender Necklace]]|[[Ender Necklace]]|Has a 0.5% chance to drop from an [[Enderman]].|
|[[Ender Cloak]]|[[Ender Cloak]]|Has a 1% chance to drop from a [[Seer]].|
|[[Ender Belt]]|[[Ender Belt]]|Has a 1% chance to drop from an [[Obsidian Defender]].|
|[[Ender Gauntlet]]|[[Ender Gauntlet]]|Has a 3.32% chance to drop from an [[End Stone]] Ender Node ([[Ender Nodes]]) and a 3.05% chance to drop from an [[Obsidian]] Ender Node ([[Ender Nodes]]).|
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Ender Helmet ([[Ender Armor]]); [[Health]]: +20 [[Defense]]: +35 All Stats of this [[Armor]] are doubled while on the [[End Island]]|Ender Chestplate ([[Ender Armor]]); [[Health]]: +30 [[Defense]]: +60 All Stats of this [[Armor]] are doubled while on the [[End Island]]|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Ender Leggings ([[Ender Armor]]); [[Health]]: +25 [[Defense]]: +50 All Stats of this [[Armor]] are doubled while on the [[End Island]]|Ender Boots ([[Ender Armor]]); [[Health]]: +15 [[Defense]]: +25 All Stats of this [[Armor]] are doubled while on the [[End Island]]|
|Necklace|Cloak|
|[[Ender Necklace]]; [[Health]]: +15 [[Defense]]: +10 All stats on this piece of equipment are multiplied by 2x while on the End Island!|[[Ender Cloak]]; [[Health]]: +15 [[Defense]]: +10 All stats on this piece of equipment are multiplied by 2x while on the End Island!|
|Belt|Gloves|
|[[Ender Belt]]; [[Health]]: +15 [[Defense]]: +10 All stats on this piece of equipment are multiplied by 2x while on the End Island!|[[Ender Gauntlet]]; [[Health]]: +15 [[Defense]]: +10 All stats on this piece of equipment are multiplied by 2x while on the End Island!|
[Total]
[Ender Armor Health: +150 Defense: +210]
The stats of each piece are multiplied by 2x in [[The End]]. This affect base stats, [[Reforging|Reforges]], [[Enchantments]], [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]], [[Fuming Potato Book|Fuming Potato Books]], etc. A Minimum of [[Combat|Combat XII (12)]] is required to use this set.
A [[Void Sword]] can be used with Ender Armor to gain up to +160 [[Strength]] whilst wearing all Ender Armor and [[Equipment]] pieces.
## Tips
- All pieces of the Ender Armor set can be sold to any NPC for a price of 10,000 [[Coins]], making collecting the armor pieces and selling them an effective early game money making method, especially when paired with mining [[Ender Nodes]] for [[Mite Gel]].
- The set is recommended for the early-game due to its high damage and the fact that most early-game players will spend a lot of time in [[The End]].
- Reforging the Armor to [[Reforging|Fierce]] will buff the player's [[Crit Damage]] and [[Crit Chance]] immensely.
- Ender Armor is often used alongside a [[Raider Sword]], an [[Aspect of the End]], or a [[Void Sword]].
- Reforging the Equipment to Stained, Honored or Blended will give the player a sizeable increase in [[Crit Chance]].
## Trivia
- Before the [[Final Destination Armor]] was buffed on February 2, 2022, some players would use Ender Armor against the [[Voidgloom Seraph]] due to the stats multiplier applying to reforges such as [[Reforging|Ancient]].

|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Lvl 42 Endermen now have a 1/1000 chance to drop Ender Armor (from 0).|
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Lvl 45 Endermen now have a 1/3000 chance to drop Ender Armor (from 1/1000).|
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Lvl 50 Endermen now have a 1/5000 chance to drop Ender Armor (from 1/1000).|

Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENDER_ARTIFACT
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +12; Reduces the damage taken from  Ender mobs by 20%.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
The Ender Artifact is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that can be bought from the [[Dark Auction]].
## Obtaining
### Dark Auction
The Ender Artifact has a chance to appear in round 1, 3 or 4 of the [[Dark Auction]] with starting bid of 20,000,000 [[Coins]].
### Raffle of the Century
Ender Artifact can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Ender Artifact
[Effects]
[Reduces the damage taken from Ender mobs by 20%.]
This accessory is quite important to have when on [[The End]] island for fighting higher tiers of the [[Voidgloom Seraph]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ender Relic]]|Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|[[Ender Artifact]] 1x Ender Artifact 1x [[Exceedingly Rare Ender Artifact Upgrade]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Obsidian]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Obsidian]] x32 ×4 + [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]] x32 ×3 + Ender Artifact + [[Exceedingly Rare Ender Artifact Upgrade]] → [[Ender Relic]]|
## Trivia
- The head's texture is based on the fireball the Ender Dragon shoots at you during vanilla dragon fights.
- There was once a bug where you could stack the [[Ender Relic]] with the Ender Artifact to get 45% reduced damage taken from End mobs.
  - This was patched in the 2021 Oct 5 update.
  - Players were able to get 87% damage reduction from Endermen with an [[Enderman Pet]] and [[Atomsplit Katana]] held.

Item ID: ENIGMA_CLOAK
Tooltip: Find Enigma Souls across the Rift Dimension.; Each adds +20 Rift Time to this item.; Every 4 Souls, go back to Enigma for more upgrades.; You found: 0\ 52 Souls; Right-click to equip!; EPIC CLOAK
The Enigma Cloak is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Cloaks|Cloak]].
## Obtaining
The Enigma Cloak can be claimed from [[Enigma]] for 100 [[Motes]].
## Usage
Cloak:
Enigma Cloak
[Effects]
[Find Enigma Souls across the Rift Dimension.; Each adds +20 Rift Time to this item.; Every 4 Souls, go back to Enigma Enigma for more upgrades.]
For each Enigma Soul the player finds, an additional +20 [[Rift Time]] will be added to the cloak. Additionally, every 4 Enigma Souls allows the player to talk to [[Enigma]] to purchase +2 [[Speed]] and +4% [[Mana Regen]].
### Enigma Souls
[[Enigma Souls]] can be found across the [[Rift Dimension]], in all sorts of places. Discovering an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] will result in the following messages:
SOUL! You unlocked an Enigma Soul!
You have found #/52 Souls in the Rift Dimension!
Your Enigma Cloak now has +#ф Rift Time!
For each [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] found, an additional +20 [[Rift Time]] is added to the Enigma Cloak, capped at 1,040 once having discovered all 52/52 [[Enigma Souls]]. An additional +26 [[Speed (Rift)|Rift Speed]] and +52% [[Mana Regen]] can be purchased from [[Enigma]].

# Entropy Suppressor
Aliases: Coldfused
Categories: Epic items, Reforge stones, Craftable items, Chili Pepper Collection
[Entropy Suppressor]

Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])

Applied To: [[Firedust Dagger]], [[Twilight Dagger]] and all of their upgrades
Apply Cost: 250,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XX (20) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

Auctionable: ✕ No

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 1,024x (16 stacks (1,024 items total)) [[Crude Gabagool]] 1,320x (20 stacks plus 40 (1,320 in total)) [[Sulphur]] 21,120x (330 stacks (21,120 items total)) [[Coal]] 32x [[Chili Pepper]] 4x [[Kelvin Inverter]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENTROPY_SUPPRESSOR
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Coldfused reforge when combined with a Melee Weapon.; Can be used with Blaze Slayer daggers.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Crit Damage  Magic Find; Coldfused Bonus While a Wisp Pet is active:; +75 Strength +55 Crit Damage; Deal +1 Damage to Fire Pillars. Breaking one grants +30 True Defense and +15%  Damage for 60s.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 20.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
The Entropy Suppressor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Coldfused]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Blaze Slayer]] daggers. Its recipe is unlocked at [[Chili Pepper|Chili Pepper IV (4)]]. It grants [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]] and [[Magic Find]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Chili Pepper IV (4) ([[Chili Pepper]])|4x [[Kelvin Inverter]] 16x [[Heavy Gabagool]] 32x [[Chili Pepper]]|Recipe: [[Chili Pepper]] x8 ×4 + [[Heavy Gabagool]] x4 ×4 + [[Kelvin Inverter]] x4 → Entropy Suppressor|
## Usage
### Reforging
Entropy Suppressor can be combined with a [[Firedust Dagger]], [[Twilight Dagger]] and all of their upgrades using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Coldfused]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]], which scale with the Daggers's rarity and 2 [[Magic Find]]. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XX (20)]]. A Dagger that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Coldfused]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Coldfused Bonus, which makes the item deal +1 [[Damage]] against [[Inferno Demonlord]]'s Fire Pillars and grants the player +15% [[Damage]] and 30 [[True Defense]] for 60s after breaking a Fire Pillar.

|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|

## Trivia
- The Entropy Suppressor is unique amongst all reforge stones since it can only be applied to a specific [[Sword Types|Sword Type]].
- Entropy is a scientific concept as well as a measurable physical property that is most commonly associated with a state of disorder, randomness, or uncertainty.
  - It is implied that the way the Entropy Suppressor suppresses entropy is by giving [[Magic Find]] randomness, outside of the [[Rift Dimension]], it is pre-defined and only affected by factors such as [[Magic Find]], according to [[Tel Kar]].

An Ethereal Vine is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item used to unlock more space in the [[Greenhouse]].
## Obtaining
An Ethereal Vine can be found when harvesting a [[Mutations|Mutation]] in the [[Greenhouse]]. The odds depend on [[Mutations|Mutation]] rarity and are as follows:
|Mutation Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|15%|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|20%|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|25%|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|30%|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|40%|
Additionally, Ethereal Vines have a 5% (1 in 20) chance to be found when harvesting a crop with additional rolls.
## Usage
An Ethereal Vine can be used in the [[Greenhouse]] to unlock more slots. While holding an Etheral Vine, all locked slots on the Greenhouse become red or green terracotta: they can be used on green terracotta, which represents a slot that is adjacent to an unlocked slot. When an Ethereal Vine is used, the following text appears:
You use the Ethereal Vine to free the rooted space, unlocking
a new planting slot!
### Garden Upgrades
|Plant Slot or Greenhouse Slot|Cost|Cumulative Cost|
|-|-|-|
|Greenhouse Slot #2|[[Ethereal Vine]] 100 (1 stack plus 36) Ethereal Vines 10 Compost Bundles ([[Compost Bundle]])|[[Ethereal Vine]] 188 (2 stacks plus 60) Ethereal Vines 10 Compost Bundles ([[Compost Bundle]])|
|Greenhouse Slot #3|[[Ethereal Vine]] 150 (2 stacks plus 22) Ethereal Vines 20 Compost Bundles ([[Compost Bundle]])|[[Ethereal Vine]] 338 (5 stacks plus 18) Ethereal Vines 30 Compost Bundles ([[Compost Bundle]])|
## Tips
- If buying Ethereal Vines from the Bazaar is not an option, growing mutations with the Bonus Drop effects is a cheap and viable alternative.
  - [[Gloomgourd|Gloomgourds]] are a great option, requiring only a Pumpkin and Watermelon nearby. Ensure that many of them can grow close together to share their Bonus Drop trait with one another.

|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Ethereal Vine as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

# Etherwarp Conduit
Aliases: Ether Transmission, Ether transmission, Etherwarp
Categories: Epic items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Enderman Slayer, Tools, Other Tools
[Etherwarp Conduit]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 122,880x (1,920 stacks (122,880 items total)) [[Obsidian]] 3,072x (48 stacks (3,072 items total)) [[Null Sphere]] 40,960x (640 stacks (40,960 items total)) [[Titanium]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ETHERWARP_CONDUIT
Tooltip: Ability: Ether Transmission RIGHT CLICK Teleport to your targetted block up to 57 blocks away.; Soulflow Cost: 1; Mana Cost: 180; ☠ Requires Enderman Slayer 7.; EPIC
The Etherwarp Conduit is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item from [[Enderman Slayer]]. It teleports its user a long range.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|16x [[Refined Titanium]] 24x [[Null Ovoid]]|Recipe: [[Null Ovoid]] x3 ×8 + [[Refined Titanium]] x16 → Etherwarp Conduit|
## Usage
Item:
Etherwarp Conduit
[Effects]
[Ability: Ether Transmission RIGHT CLICK; Teleport to your targeted block up to 57 blocks away.; Soulflow Cost (Soulflow (Mechanic)): 1; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 180]
Etherwarp Conduit can be used to teleport to a block up to 57 blocks away, which can be increased via [[Transmission Tuner|Transmission Tuners]] to up to 61 blocks. The item itself can be applied [[Aspect of the End]] or [[Aspect of the Void]] using the recipe seen below. When the ability is applied to these two items, it does not require [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL VII (7)]] to be used, which is likely an oversight. When the ability is used, particles similar to Voidgloom Seraph's Hitshield spawn and rise wherever the player is standing originally. At the same time, it teleports its user to the top surface of the block they are looking at.
It can only be aimed at a block, above which there are 2 blocks. It can be used to teleport through one block ceiling, which can be used in Dungeons. Similarly to other mobility abilities, it does not work in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] boss room. The ability has no cooldown and costs 1 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] and 180 [[Mana]] to use.
### Crafting
It can be combined with an [[Aspect of the End]] or [[Aspect of the Void]] using an [[Etherwarp Merger]]. This transfers its ability to the weapon, allowing its user to use it by right clicking while sneaking. Transferring its ability requires [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL VII (7)]], however using the resulting [[Aspect of the End]] or [[Aspect of the Void]] does not.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Aspect of the End]]|Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x [[Etherwarp Merger]] [[Etherwarp Conduit]] 1x Etherwarp Conduit 1x [[Aspect of the End]]|Recipe: [[Aspect of the End]] + [[Etherwarp Merger]] + Etherwarp Conduit → [[Aspect of the End]]|
|[[Aspect of the Void]]|Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x [[Etherwarp Merger]] [[Etherwarp Conduit]] 1x Etherwarp Conduit 1x [[Aspect of the Void]]|Recipe: [[Aspect of the Void]] + [[Etherwarp Merger]] + Etherwarp Conduit → [[Aspect of the Void]]|
## Trivia
- Its ability can pass through 1-block thick floors from the bottom.
- This item's texture is based on the Conduit in Vanilla Minecraft.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 31, 2021|0.11.5|Added Etherwarp Conduit.|
|Jun 6, 2021|Ability: Ether Transmission no longer works in the boss room of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].|
|Unknown Date|Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]]) is now required to apply it to an item.|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ETHERWARP_MERGER
Tooltip: Crafted by the Etherchemist and dropped by the Enderseraph.; Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC
An Etherwarp Merger is a [[Rarity|EPIC]] item. It is a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] drop from the [[Voidgloom Seraph]] Tier IV. It is used to combine the [[Etherwarp Conduit]] with an [[Aspect of the End]] or [[Aspect of the Void]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
The Etherwarp Merger is a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] drop from the [[Voidgloom Seraph]]. It has a 0.42% (1/236.15) chance to drop from Tier IV [[Voidgloom Seraph]] if the player has unlocked [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer VII (7)]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Aspect of the End]]|Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|[[Etherwarp Merger]] 1x Etherwarp Merger 1x [[Etherwarp Conduit]] 1x [[Aspect of the End]]|Recipe: [[Aspect of the End]] + Etherwarp Merger + [[Etherwarp Conduit]] → [[Aspect of the End]]|
|[[Aspect of the Void]]|Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|[[Etherwarp Merger]] 1x Etherwarp Merger 1x [[Etherwarp Conduit]] 1x [[Aspect of the Void]]|Recipe: [[Aspect of the Void]] + Etherwarp Merger + [[Etherwarp Conduit]] → [[Aspect of the Void]]|
## Trivia
- While the [[Etherwarp Conduit]] requires [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer VII (7)]] to use, an [[Aspect of the End]] or [[Aspect of the Void]] combined with an [[Etherwarp Conduit]] does not.

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 204,800x (3,200 stacks (204,800 items total)) [[Coal]] 51,200x (800 stacks (51,200 items total)) [[Red Sand]] 51,200x [[Sulphur]] 1,920x (30 stacks (1,920 items total)) [[Iron Ingot]] 16x [[Flames]] 3x [[Heat Core]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EVERBURNING_FLAME
Tooltip: Minion Fuel; Increases the speed of your minion by 35%. Unlimited Duration!; Everburning Bonus: 5% extra speed on Combat minions.; EPIC
An Everburning Flame is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Minion Fuel]] that increases [[Minions|Minion]] speed.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Red Sand VIII (8) ([[Red Sand]])|1x [[Plasma Bucket]] 2x [[Enchanted Red Sand Cube]] 2x [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] 16x [[Flames]]|Recipe: [[Flames]] x4 ×4 + [[Enchanted Red Sand Cube]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] ×2 + [[Plasma Bucket]] → Everburning Flame|
## Usage
### Minion Fuel
An Everburning Flame increases [[Minions|Minion]] speed by 35% and increases [[Minions|Combat Minion]] speed by an additional 5%, meaning that combat minions get a 40% speed boost. Everburning Flame can be placed in all [[Minions]].
It has an unlimited duration and can be taken from the minion at any time, without being destroyed. It is currently the most efficient infinite fuel source for Combat Minions.
### Forging
An Everburning Flame can be used to forge a [[Will-o'-wisp]].
- 1 [[Will-o'-wisp]]
  - 1 [[Glacite Lantern]]
  - 1 [[Caged Wisp]]
  - 1 [[Perfect Plate]]
  - [[Everburning Flame]] 1 Everburning Flame
  - 8 [[Enchanted Emerald Block]]
## Bugs
- Everburning Flame's +5% bonus for [[Minions|Combat Minions]] does not apply to [[Spider Minion|Spider Minions]].

Categories: Epic items, Items, Craftable items, Vinesap Collection, Tools
[Everstretch Lasso]

Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]

Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EVERSTRETCH_LASSO
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Ability: Throw RIGHT CLICK Throw the Lasso at Forest creatures and hold them still until they lose all their stamina.; Ability: Reel In RIGHT CLICK Reel in creatures caught by the Lasso each time their stamina depletes.;  Critters lose stamina 60% faster.; Ability: Wrangle Grants +5 Hunting Fortune on Forest Shards caught with this Lasso.; EPIC LASSO
An Everstretch Lasso is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Lassos|Lasso]], whose recipe is unlocked at [[Vinesap|Vinesap VI (6)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Vinesap VI (6) ([[Vinesap]])|1x [[Entangler Lasso]] 3x [[Enchanted Vinesap]] 8x [[Mangcore]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Vinesap]] ×3 + [[Entangler Lasso]] + [[Mangcore]] x8 → Everstretch Lasso|
## Usage
An Everstretch Lasso can be thrown at forest creatures to catch them. While held, a stamina bar appears above its head. When the bar reaches 20%, a prompt to reel appears. Right-clicking will deplete the stamina bar and pull in the mob.
An Everstretch Lasso depletes the stamina bars on [[Critters|Critter]] type mobs 60% faster.
An Everstretch Lasso also grants +5 [[Hunting Fortune]] on Forest shards caught with this lasso.

A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; While equipped in an Exp Share slot this pet gains +15% Exp Share rate.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; EPIC PET ITEM

### Crafting

# Explosive Bow
Categories: Epic items, Weapons, Craftable items, Gunpowder Collection, Bows
[Explosive Bow]
Type: Bow ([[Bows]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Gunpowder IX (9) ([[Gunpowder]])
Stats: [[Damage]]: +100; [[Strength]]: +20
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Sell: 62,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 30,720x (480 stacks (30,720 items total)) [[Gunpowder]] 48x [[Sugar Cane]] 3x [[String]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EXPLOSIVE_BOW
Tooltip: Damage: +100; Strength: +20; Ability: Explosive Shot Creates an explosion on impact!; Every Monster caught in this explosion takes the full damage of the weapon!; Acts as Superboom TNT!; This item can be reforged!; EPIC BOW
The Explosive Bow is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]] unlocked at [[Gunpowder|Gunpowder IX (9)]]. Its Explosive Shot Ability makes [[Arrows]] explode on impact, dealing damage to all [[Mob|Mobs]] within range.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Gunpowder IX (9) ([[Gunpowder]])|3x [[Enchanted Firework Rocket]] 3x [[String]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Firework Rocket]] ×3 + [[String]] ×3 → Explosive Bow|
## Usage
Bow:
Explosive Bow [[Damage]]: +100 [[Strength]]: +20
[Effects]
[Ability: Explosive Shot; Creates an explosion on impact! Every Monster caught in this explosion takes the full damage of the weapon!; Acts as Superboom TNT!]
The Explosive Bow's arrows explode on impact. All enemies within 3 blocks of the arrow take [[Damage Calculation|Projectile Damage]].
## Tips
- It is one of the 5 bows which can damage [[Enderman|Endermen]] and [[Zealot|Zealots]], the others being [[Spider Queen's Stinger]], [[Bonemerang]], [[Juju Shortbow]] and [[Terminator]].
- The Explosive Bow is great for goblin commissions in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. The AOE damage of the bow allows players to quickly kill goblins.
- Sword Swapping to another weapon to boost damage does not work anymore due to sword swapping for projectiles being patched.
- The Explosive Bow can be used as a substitute for [[Superboom TNT]] as the explosion can destroy tombs and breakable walls.
- If [[Piercing]] is applied, the arrow will not explode on the first enemy hit.
  - This allows it to deal double damage if the arrow explosion is in range of the hit enemy.
## History

|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|The explosion now works like [[Superboom TNT]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Explosive Bow as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

# Farming for Dummies
Aliases: Farming For Dummies, Farming for
Categories: Epic items, Items, The Uprising, Farming
[Farming for Dummies]
Type: Book
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Sell: 100,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FARMING_FOR_DUMMIES
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Grants +1 Farming Fortune when applied to a Hoe, Axe, or Vacuum. Can be applied to an item up to 5 times.; EPIC
Farming for Dummies is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Book Modifier.
## Obtaining
Farming for Dummies has a 4.83% (1 in 20.71 pigs) chance to be dropped by [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] during the [[Year of the Pig]].
## Usage
Farming for Dummies can be applied to a [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]] or a [[Vacuums|Vacuum]] up to 5 times via an [[Anvil]] in order to grant it +1 [[Farming Fortune]] per application.
The additional [[Farming Fortune]] added to the item from Farming for Dummies will be displayed in light green parentheses next to the stat, like so: (+[#]).
## Trivia
- Farming for Dummies references [[The Great Potato War]], where YouTubers Technoblade and im_a_squid_kid competed to be the first to reach a [[Potato]] [[Collections|Collection]] of 500 million, which Technoblade won.
- The item's name references the For Dummies series of instructional books.
- For a few months after it first came out, there was a glitch where having a compatible item with Farming for Dummies in the player's inventory would still boost their [[Farming Fortune]] even when not holding the item.
## History

## Obtaining
### Crafting

|-|-|-|

## Usage
Sword:
Fel Sword [[Damage]]: +190 [[Strength]]: +135
[Effects]
[Every 100 Kills with this sword grants +1 Weapon Damage, up to 100 extra.; Current Kills: 0 (+0 Damage); - Increases healing from your Healing Circle by 10%.; - Reduces the cooldown of your Wish Ultimate by 10 seconds.; Right-click to use Class Ability!]
The Fel Sword, being a sword, can be used for melee damage. For every 100 kills the wielder accumulates using a Fel Sword, it will gain +1 [[Damage]]: this caps at +100 [[Damage]], or 10,000 kills.
While playing as a Healer in the [[Catacombs]], the Fel Sword buffs the Healing Circle's healing rate by +10% and reduces the cooldown of Wish by 10s.

- Accumulated damage from the Fel Sword's damage bonus does not appear in its tooltip. Additionally, it is not affected by [[Catacombs]] levels, [[Star Upgrades]], or any similar modifiers.

The Fenlord Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Berry Mogul]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Fenlord Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Bullfrog Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher and Forest Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Fenlord Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Berry Mogul]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Berry Mogul ([[Attributes]]); Your Salts last +1%–10% longer.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Fenlord Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Fenlord Shard has the following description:
- These legendary amphibians are both feared and revered, known for their immense size and territorial nature.

# Fermento
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Fermento]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Condensed Fermento]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Garden Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 250,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FERMENTO
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC
Fermento is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item used to craft [[Condensed Fermento]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
- [[Fermento]] Fermento has a chance to drop when breaking a mature [[Sugar Cane]], [[Cactus]], [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]], [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]], or [[Nether Wart]] crop while wearing at least 2 pieces of [[Squash Armor]], [[Fermento Armor]], or [[Helianthus Armor]].
- Pieces from different armor sets do not count towards each other's Tiered Bonus.
- The chance can be increased by [[Overbloom]].
- For [[Sugar Cane]] and [[Cactus]], only the block directly broken rolls for [[Fermento]] Fermento.
Pieces: Chance
1/4: 0.000% (0 in ∞ crops broken)
2/4: 0.005% (1 in 20,000.0 crops broken)
3/4: 0.006% (1 in 16,666.6 crops broken)
4/4: 0.007% (1 in 14,285.7 crops broken)
### Greenhouse
- [[Fermento]] Fermento has a chance to drop when breaking any crop or mutation with a "Harvestable" status inside the [[Greenhouse]] while wearing at least 2 pieces of [[Squash Armor]], [[Fermento Armor]], or [[Helianthus Armor]].
- Pieces from different armor sets do not count towards each other's Tiered Bonus.
- The chance can be increased by [[Overbloom]].
Pieces: Chance
1/4: 0.0% (0 in ∞ crops broken)
2/4: 2% (1 in 50 crops broken)
3/4: 2.4% (1 in 41.6 crops broken)
4/4: 2.8% (1 in 35.7 crops broken)
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Condensed Fermento]]|None|[[Fermento]] 9x Fermento|Recipe: Fermento ×9 → [[Condensed Fermento]]|
|[[Garden Sack]]|None|1x [[Enchanted Leather]] 4x [[Box of Seeds]] [[Fermento]] 4x Fermento|Recipe: [[Box of Seeds]] ×4 + Fermento ×4 + [[Enchanted Leather]] → [[Garden Sack]]|
### Composter
Fermento can be used to fill up the [[Composter]]'s Crop Meter by 20k Organic Matter.
It can also be used to purchase [[Composter|Composter Upgrades]] tiers 20 to 22
|Tier|Amount required|
|-|-|
|20|[[Fermento]] 3 Fermento|
|21|[[Fermento]] 6 Fermento|
|22|[[Fermento]] 12 Fermento|
### Mutations
Fermento is required to grow [[Cheesebite]] in the [[Greenhouse]].

Fermento Armor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] set that combines the Tiered Bonus of [[Tater Armor]], [[Cropie Armor]] and [[Squash Armor]], as well as granting a chance to drop a [[Helianthus]]. It requires [[Farming|Farming XL (40)]] to use.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x Squash Helmet ([[Squash Armor]]) 2x [[Condensed Fermento]] 16x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] 16x [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] 32x [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]]|Recipe: [[Condensed Fermento]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] x32 + [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] x16 + [[Squash Armor]] + [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] x16 → [[Fermento Armor]]|
|None|1x Squash Chestplate ([[Squash Armor]]) 2x [[Condensed Fermento]] 16x [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] 16x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] 25x [[Enchanted Cactus]] 30x [[Mutant Nether Wart]] 32x [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]]|Recipe: [[Condensed Fermento]] ×2 + [[Squash Armor]] + [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] x16 + [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] x32 + [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] x16 + [[Mutant Nether Wart]] x15 ×2 + [[Enchanted Cactus]] x25 → [[Fermento Armor]]|
|None|1x Squash Leggings ([[Squash Armor]]) 2x [[Condensed Fermento]] 16x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] 16x [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] 32x [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] 40x [[Mutant Nether Wart]]|Recipe: [[Condensed Fermento]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] x32 + [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] x16 + [[Squash Armor]] + [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] x16 + [[Mutant Nether Wart]] x20 ×2 → [[Fermento Armor]]|
|None|1x Squash Boots ([[Squash Armor]]) 2x [[Condensed Fermento]] 16x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] 16x [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]]|Recipe: [[Condensed Fermento]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] x16 + [[Squash Armor]] + [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] x16 → [[Fermento Armor]]|
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Fermento Helmet ([[Fermento Armor]]); [[Health]]: +130 [[Defense]]: +40 [[Speed]]: +5 [[Bonus Pest Chance]]: +17.5 [[Farming Fortune]]: +30|Fermento Chestplate ([[Fermento Armor]]); [[Health]]: +195 [[Defense]]: +40 [[Speed]]: +6 [[Bonus Pest Chance]]: +17.5 [[Farming Fortune]]: +35|

[Total]
[Fermento Armor Health: +650 Defense: +160 Speed: +22 Bonus Pest Chance: +70 Farming Fortune: +130]
[Effects]
[Tiered Bonus: Feast; Farming Wild Rose, Sunflower and Moonflower have a 0.000% (1 Piece)➜0.002% (2 Pieces)➜0.003% (3 Pieces)➜0.004% (4 Pieces) chance of dropping a Helianthus. Inside the Greenhouse gives a 0.0%➜0.8%➜1.2%➜1.6% chance to drop one from any crop grown inside. Also combines the Tiered Bonuses of wearing 1➜2➜3➜4 pieces of the Tater Armor, Cropie Armor, and Squash Armor. Grants +0➜25➜50➜75 Farming Fortune.; Pieces Required: 2+]
When wearing Fermento Armor and collecting crops, there is a chance to harvest rare crops depending on the Piece Bonus and crop. [[Cropie]] is dropped by [[Wheat]], [[Carrot]] and [[Potato]], [[Squash]] is dropped by [[Pumpkin]], [[Melon]], or [[Cocoa Beans]], [[Fermento]] is dropped by [[Sugar Cane]], [[Cactus]], [[Mushroom]], and [[Nether Wart]], and [[Helianthus]] is dropped by [[Sunflower]], [[Moonflower]], [[Wild Rose]] and [[Greenhouse|Greenhouse Crops]].

|Helianthus Helmet ([[Helianthus Armor]])|None|1x Fermento Helmet ([[Fermento Armor]]) 2x [[Condensed Helianthus]] 64x [[Compacted Sunflower]] 64x [[Compacted Wild Rose]] 64x [[Compacted Moonflower]]|Recipe: [[Condensed Helianthus]] ×2 + [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x64 + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x64 + [[Fermento Armor]] + [[Compacted Moonflower]] x64 → [[Helianthus Armor]]|
|Helianthus Chestplate ([[Helianthus Armor]])|None|1x Fermento Chestplate ([[Fermento Armor]]) 2x [[Condensed Helianthus]] 64x [[Compacted Sunflower]] 64x [[Compacted Wild Rose]] 64x [[Compacted Moonflower]]|Recipe: [[Condensed Helianthus]] ×2 + [[Fermento Armor]] + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x32 ×2 + [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x32 ×2 + [[Compacted Moonflower]] x32 ×2 → [[Helianthus Armor]]|
|Helianthus Leggings ([[Helianthus Armor]])|None|1x Fermento Leggings ([[Fermento Armor]]) 2x [[Condensed Helianthus]] 64x [[Compacted Sunflower]] 64x [[Compacted Wild Rose]] 64x [[Compacted Moonflower]]|Recipe: [[Condensed Helianthus]] ×2 + [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x64 + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x32 ×2 + [[Fermento Armor]] + [[Compacted Moonflower]] x32 ×2 → [[Helianthus Armor]]|
|Helianthus Boots ([[Helianthus Armor]])|None|1x Fermento Boots ([[Fermento Armor]]) 2x [[Condensed Helianthus]] 64x [[Compacted Sunflower]] 64x [[Compacted Moonflower]]|Recipe: [[Condensed Helianthus]] ×2 + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x64 + [[Fermento Armor]] + [[Compacted Moonflower]] x64 → [[Helianthus Armor]]|

## Trivia
- In its [[Crafting]] recipe, Fermento Armor was displayed as needing 33x of the previous [[Squash Armor]] piece, with 32 of them in a stack. This could crash people's game very easily and was fixed to become 32 Enchanted Brown Mushroom Blocks.
- Wearing a full set of Fermento Armor gives the [[Achievements|"Forbidden Fruit" Achievement]].
- The Fermento Helmet has the same design as the [[Crimson Armor|Crimson Helmet]], [[Armor of Divan|Helmet of Divan]], and [[Warden Helmet]].
  - As with other three helmets that share this design, this armor is geared towards a specific skill.

A Fertilized Jerryseed is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
A Fertilized Jerryseed can be obtained by talking to Talking to [[Dirt Guy]] with an [[Unripe Jerryseed]] [[Reforging|Reforged]] to [[Reforging|Dirty]].
## Usage
A Fertilized Jerryseed can be planted on farmland in the [[Greenhouse]] to grow a [[Jerryflower]]. Upon reaching stage 5, the Fertilized Jerryseed stops growing until given 10 [[Move Jerry]].

A Festering Maggot is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Consumables|Permanent Consumable]] item used to increase the player's [[Defense]].
## Obtaining
A Festering Maggot has a 0.5% (1 in 200) chance to drop from a Tier IV or above [[Revenant Horror]] once at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VI (6)]].
## Usage
Consuming a Festering Maggot grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+2 SkyBlock XP]] and a permanent +1 [[Defense]]. Up to 5 Festering Maggots can be consumed in total.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|First Maggot|ARGH! Things don't seem too bad at first, but then the maggot explodes in your mouth.|
|Second Maggot|PHEW! Better hold your nose while chewing, the smell isn't helping things.|
|Third Maggot|UGH! The third tastes three times worse than the second, which tasted twice as bad as the first. Things aren't looking up. But hey, anything for a small stat buff.|
|Fourth Maggot|HIC! You struggle to keep a burp down, and it tastes like more maggots. Nasty.|
|Fifth Maggot|NO MORE! Not again, not even once. You say that, but we both know you'll do anything for a small stat boost. Be it maggot, larva, or vial, you'll be there, hungry for more.|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|You've already consumed the maximum amount of this item!|
## Trivia
- "Be it maggot, larva, or vial, you'll be there, hungry for more." references the [[Wriggling Larva]] and [[Vial of Venom]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 8, 2025|0.23.1|Added Festering Maggot.|
|Jul 17, 2025|0.23.2 RC|Changed Festering Maggot granting bonus Undead ([[Undead Items]]) [[Damage]] to 1 [[Defense]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 22, 2025|0.23.2|Added Festering Maggot.|
|May 6, 2026|0.24.5|Moved Festering Maggot from Extra loottable of Slayer Mobs to Main.|

The Field Mouse Despair Rat Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Field Mouse Despair Rat Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on December 7, 2024. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 10,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Field Mouse Despair Rat Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Rat Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

Shard Item ID: FIRE_EEL
Tooltip: Trophy Hunter I Grants +2  Trophy Chance.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; EPIC WATER SHARD (ID E14)
The Fire Eel Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Trophy Hunter]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Fire Eel Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Fire Eel]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Kuudra
A Fire Eel Shard can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Loot]].
### Fusion
A Fire Eel Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Moray Eel Shard]]
- Second: [[Eel Shard]], [[Firefly Shard]], or [[Firefox Shard]]
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Fire Eel Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Trophy Hunter]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Trophy Hunter ([[Attributes]]); Grants +2–20 [[Trophy Chance]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Fire Eel Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Fire Eel Shard has the following description:
- They are known for their feisty behavior, often flaring their fins and displaying their colors when threatened.
## Trivia
- Despite being an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Fire Eel Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

# Firefly Shard
Categories: Epic items, Shards

Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: FIREFLY
Tooltip: Solar Power I Grants +5  Farming Fortune during the day.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 15.; EPIC FOREST SHARD (ID E31)
The Firefly Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Solar Power]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Firefly Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Firefly]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Firefly Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Praying Mantis Shard]]
- Second: [[Bezal Shard]], [[Lava Flame Shard]], [[Fire Eel Shard]], [[Bal Shard]], [[Flare Shard]], [[Cinderbat Shard]], or [[Flaming Spider Shard]]
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Firefly Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Solar Power]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Solar Power ([[Attributes]]); Grants +5–50 [[Farming Fortune]] during the day.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Firefly Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Firefly Shard has the following description:
- Fireflies illuminate the night with their fiery glow, their small bodies engulfed in a flickering flame.

The Firmitas Glyph is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Quest Item obtained in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
The Firmitas Glyph can be obtained after starting [[Sorcerer Okron]]'s quest and imbuing the [[Robur Glyph]], then opening the chest at [[Coordinates|-94.5, 77, -85.5]] in the [[Cake House]]. Once given to Okron, it can no longer be obtained.
## Usage
The Firmitas Glyph can be given to [[Sorcerer Okron]]. When given, he will task the player with using the [[Warding Diamathame]] to imbue a spell onto the house.

### Crafting

|Yog Helmet ([[Yog Armor]])|Hard Stone V (5) ([[Hard Stone]])|1x Flamebreaker Helmet ([[Flamebreaker Armor]]) 40x [[Yoggie]]|Recipe: [[Yoggie]] x5 ×8 + [[Flamebreaker Armor]] → [[Yog Armor]]|

## Tips
- Due to its subpar stats and the low cost of upgrading, it is recommended to upgrade Flamebreaker Armor to [[Yog Armor]].
## Trivia
- Flamebreaker Armor was originally released alongside the [[Silex]] for a day prior to the release of the [[Crystal Hollows]].
  - On the release of the [[Crystal Hollows]], existing pieces of Flamebreaker Armor were converted to [[Ember Ash Armor]].
  - The [[Ember Ash Armor|Ember Ash Helmet]] and [[Ember Ash Armor|Ember Ash Leggings]] shared a recipe, making them unobtainable to players without a rank.
- Placing a [[Gemstone|Ruby]] in Flamebreaker Armor and upgrading it into [[Yog Armor]] will cause the [[Gemstone|Ruby]] to stop granting [[Health]]. It will be available for refund at the [[Geo|Gemstone Grinder]].

## Bugs
- Two of the three projectiles spawned by the Flaming Fist remain stationary.
- The Flaming Fist projectiles can be triggered by attacking another player, NPC, or Cosmetic on the [[Private Island]].
  - The projectiles will also trigger when the [[Juju Shortbow]] hits an [[Enderman]].
- The Flaming Fist projectiles count as bats for the [[Bat Person Artifact]].
- The Flaming Fist projectiles uses the [[Very Crude Gabagool]] texture.
- The Flaming Fist reduces the hits required in the [[Belly of the Beast]] from three to one. This is due to each projectile counting as a hit.
- The Flaming Fist used to be able to damage [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] Villagers on other players [[Private Island]].

The Flare Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Infernal Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Flare Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Flare]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Kuudra
A Flare Shard can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Loot]].
### Fusion
A Flare Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Flare Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Infernal Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Infernal Ruler ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3%–30% more [[Damage]] against Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]]) mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Flare Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Flare Shard has the following description:
- Capable of igniting the air itself, Flare can generate intense heat waves, making the very atmosphere around it shimmer and distort.
## Trivia
- Despite being an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Flare Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

Sell: 500,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FLY_SWATTER
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC
A Fly Swatter is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that can drop rarely from Tier III+ [[Tarantula Broodfather|Tarantula Broodfathers]] upon reaching [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer V (5)]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
A Fly Swatter is a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] drop from Tier III+ [[Tarantula Broodfather|Tarantula Broodfathers]] upon reaching [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer V (5)]].
Drop Chances:
- Tier III: 0.08% (1/1212)
- Tier IV: 0.17% (1/601.1)
- Tier V: 0.21% (1/476)[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Flycatcher]]|Spider Slayer VI (6) ([[Spider Slayer]])|[[Fly Swatter]] 1x Fly Swatter 102x (1 stack plus 38 (102 in total)) [[Tarantula Silk]]|Recipe: [[Tarantula Silk]] x17 ×6 + Fly Swatter → [[Flycatcher]]|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Fly Swatter as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Added Fly Swatter.|
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Can be sold to an NPC for 500,000 [[Coins]].|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Fly Swatter to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Fly Swatter as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

The Fortis Glyph is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Quest Item obtained in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
The Fortis Glyph can be obtained after starting [[Sorcerer Okron]]'s quest and imbueing the [[Validus Glyph]], then opening the chest at [[Coordinates|-77.5, 71, -148.5]] in the windmill within the [[Dreadfarm]]. Once given to Okron, it can no longer be obtained.
## Usage
The Fortis Glyph can be given to [[Sorcerer Okron]]. When given, he will task the player with using the [[Warding Diamathame]] to imbue a spell onto the house.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|You found Fortis Glyph! You should return it to Okron...|Finding the Glyph|
|You already have one in your inventory!|Trying to claim another Glyph while already owning one|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FROZEN_BAUBLE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Festive reforge when combined with Armor.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Intelligence  Fishing Speed  Sea Creature Chance; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 20.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Frozen Bauble is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Festive]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]]. It grants [[Intelligence]], [[Fishing Speed]], and [[Sea Creature Chance]].
## Obtaining
Frozen Bauble can be purchased from [[Terry]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]] for 64 (1 stack) [[Hunk of Ice]] and 32 [[Hunk of Blue Ice]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Frozen Bauble can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Festive]] reforge, to grant [[Intelligence]], [[Fishing Speed]], and [[Sea Creature Chance]], which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XX (20)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Festive]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|

A Fuming Potato Book is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item. It is obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] and serves as a direct upgrade to the [[Hot Potato Book]], allowing players to bypass the [[Hot Potato Book]] limit up to 5 times.
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
Fuming Potato Books have a chance to appear in [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] on any floor of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].

## Usage
Fuming Potato Books can be applied to items that have 10 [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] applied to them with an [[Anvil]]: a maximum of [[Fuming Potato Book]] 5 Fuming Potato Books can be applied to an item. The stats gained are as follows:
|Affected Stat|Number of Fuming Potato Book Uses 1|Number of Fuming Potato Book Uses 2|Number of Fuming Potato Book Uses 3|Number of Fuming Potato Book Uses 4|Number of Fuming Potato Book Uses 5|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Strength]]|+2|+4|+6|+8|+10|
|[[Damage]]|+2|+4|+6|+8|+10|
|Stat Boost|[[Fuming Potato Book]] 5 Fuming Potato Books| 10 Hot Potato Books ([[Hot Potato Book]]) + [[Fuming Potato Book]] 5 Fuming Potato Books|
|-|-|-|
|+2 [[Strength]]; +2 [[Damage]]|+10 [[Strength]]; +10 [[Damage]]|+30 [[Strength]]; +30 [[Damage]]|
|Affected Stat|Number of Fuming Potato Book Uses 1|Number of Fuming Potato Book Uses 2|Number of Fuming Potato Book Uses 3|Number of Fuming Potato Book Uses 4|Number of Fuming Potato Book Uses 5|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Defense]]|+2|+4|+6|+8|+10|
|[[Health]]|+4|+8|+12|+16|+20|
|Stat Boost|[[Fuming Potato Book]] 5 Fuming Potato Books| 10 Hot Potato Books ([[Hot Potato Book]]) + [[Fuming Potato Book]] 5 Fuming Potato Books|
|-|-|-|
|+4 [[Health]]; +2 [[Defense]]|+20 [[Health]]; +10 [[Defense]]|+60 [[Health]]; +30 [[Defense]]|
The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Blaze Pet]]'s Fusion-Style Potato perk doubles the effects of Fuming Potato Books.
## Trivia
- The first Fuming Potato Book was sold by 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] Jayavarmen to 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Refraction for 2B [[Coins]].
- There used to be a bug with the Fuming Potato Book where each book would add a bonus of +4 instead of +2, and it removed two anvil uses instead of just one. This meant that the Fuming Potato Book limit was only 8, or 3 if 10 [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] were already added.
- Fuming Potato Books used to be able to be applied in place of [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]]. If the player applied a Fuming Potato Book to an item that didn't already have 10 [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] applied, it would have the same effect as a [[Hot Potato Book]].

# Gemstone Chamber
Aliases: Gemstone Chambers, Gemstone chamber
Categories: Epic items, Items, Forged items

Type: Item

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
