[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 100x (1 stack plus 36 (100 in total)) [[Worm Membrane]] 25,600x (400 stacks (25,600 items total)) Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600x Rough Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600x Rough Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600x Rough Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) [[Sludge Juice]] 25,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GEMSTONE_CHAMBER
Tooltip: Used in the Gemstone Grinder to unlock Gemstone slots for Divan items.; EPIC
A Gemstone Chamber is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item unlocked at [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]]. It is obtained from [[The Forge]] and is used to unlock [[Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Slots]] on [[Armor of Divan]] and the [[Pendant of Divan]]. Each Gemstone Chamber takes 4 hours to forge.
## Obtaining
|Slot|Name|Requirements|Forging Tree|
|-|-|-|-|
|Gemstone Chamber|[[Gemstone Chamber]] Gemstone Chamber|HotM V (5) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|[[Gemstone Chamber]] 1 Gemstone Chamber 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Worm Membrane]] 1 [[Gemstone Mixture]] 4 Fine Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 320 (5 stacks) Flawed Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600 (400 stacks) Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 4 Fine Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 320 Flawed Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600 Rough Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 4 Fine Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 320 Flawed Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600 Rough Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 4 Fine Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 320 Flawed Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600 Rough Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 320 [[Sludge Juice]] 25,000 (390 stacks plus 40) [[Coins]]|
## Usage
A Gemstone Chamber can be used to unlock [[Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Slots]] on [[Armor of Divan]] and the [[Pendant of Divan]] in the [[Geo|Gemstone Grinder]] at [[Geo]]. Each individual Gemstone Slot requires a Gemstone Chamber to unlock: [[Gemstone Chamber]] 22 Gemstone Chambers are required to unlock every Gemstone Slot in total.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 21, 2024|Gemstone Chambers can now be applied on a [[Pendant of Divan]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 27, 2021|Added Gemstone Chamber.|
|Jul 27, 2021|Gemstone Chambers are applied to [[Armor of Divan]] pieces by combining them in an [[Anvil]]. The order in which the slots are unlocked cannot be changed.|
|Aug 17, 2021|Fixed the 2nd Amber and Jade slots not giving stats.|
|Sep 13, 2021|Now uses the new system of unlocking Gemstone slots. As a result, the order in which the slots are unlocked is no longer limited.|
|Sep 13, 2021|Changed tooltip text from "Can be applied in Anvils to some items to add Gemstone slots which can then be filled at the Gemstone Grinder" to "Used in the Gemstone Grinder to unlock Gemstone slots for Armor of Divan."|
|Apr 9, 2024|0.20|Gemstone Chambers can now be applied on a [[Pendant of Divan]].|

The Gemzie Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Devouring Spread]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Gemzie Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Gemzie]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Gemzie Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Gemzie Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Devouring Spread]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Devouring Spread ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.25–2.5 [[Gemstone Spread]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

A Gemzie Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Gemzie Shard has the following description:
- Gemzie are both immensely stealthy and incredibly greedy - large quantities of gems in one place are usually enough to coax them from their hiding spots.

## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Giant Isopod Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Giant Isopod]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Fusion
A Giant Isopod Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Giant Isopod Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Fisher's Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Fisher's Fortune ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Magic Find]] while on Fishing Islands.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Giant reforge when combined with Armor.; Super clean, great hygiene.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Health; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 25.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Giant Tooth is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Giant]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]], granting [[Health]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
One Giant Tooth can be claimed from [[Sadan|Sadan I (1)]], which is unlocked after defeating [[Sadan]] 50 times.
### Dungeon Reward Chests
A Giant Tooth can be obtained from Gold, Diamond, Emerald, Obsidian, and Bedrock [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] and its [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] variant.
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|

|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|5.3628% (1 in 18.6 runs)|6.7631% (1 in 14.8 runs)|160|50|
## Usage
### Reforging
Giant Tooth can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Giant]] reforge, to grant [[Health]], which scales with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXV (25)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Giant]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|

When hitting the ground, also creates an explosion of 3; blocks, dealing the same damage.; Mana Cost: 75; This item can be reforged!; EPIC SWORD
The Glacial Scythe is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]] unlocked at [[Ice|Ice XI (11)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Ice XI (11) ([[Ice]])|1x [[Frozen Scythe]] 40x [[Glacial Fragment]] 64x [[Hunk of Blue Ice]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Packed Ice]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x32 ×6 + [[Glacial Fragment]] x40 + [[Frozen Scythe]] + [[Hunk of Blue Ice]] x64 → Glacial Scythe|
## Usage
Sword:
Glacial Scythe [[Damage]]: +120 [[Intelligence]]: +30
[Effects]
[Ability: Ice Bolt RIGHT CLICK; Shoots 1 Ice Bolt that deals 1,500 damage and slows enemies hit for 5 seconds! When hitting the ground, also creates an explosion of 3 blocks, dealing the same damage.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 75; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.3]
The Glacial Scythe's Ice Bolt ability fires a bolt of ice in the direction the player is looking, dealing 1,500 damage and slowing mobs within 0.5 blocks of the projectile for 5s. If the ice bolt hits a block, it creates a 3 block explosion that deals its regular damage.
### Upgrading
Upgrading the Glacial Scythe grants:
- +10 [[Damage]]
- +10 [[Intelligence]]
- Ice Bolt gains +200 [[Damage]]
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Glacial Scythe]] 1x Glacial Scythe 8x [[Winter Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Winter Fragment]] ×8 + Glacial Scythe → [[Glacial Scythe]]|
## Tips
- The Ice Bolt projectile deals damage twice. Firstly when it passes an enemy, and when it hits a block, at which point it explodes for AoE damage. Therefore by aiming at mobs' legs or at mobs standing in front of a wall, you can double your damage. The explosion damage can be increased with [[Implosion Belt]], leading to a total of a 2.25x increase in damage if both hits land.
## Essence Crafting

|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|50|50|

|✪✪|15|75|
|✪✪✪|20|95|
|✪✪✪✪|25; 10,000 [[Coins]]|120; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|30; 25,000 [[Coins]]|150; 35,000 [[Coins]]|

[Total]
[Glacite Armor Defense: +415 True Defense: +30 Speed: +50 Mining Speed: +40 Mining Fortune: +20]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Expert Miner; Grants +2Mining Speed for each of your mining levels.]
Glacite Armor's Expert Miner full set bonus grants +2 [[Mining Speed]] per [[Mining]] level. Additionally, each piece grants +10 [[Mining Speed]] and +5 [[Mining Fortune]].
### Salvaging
Each piece of Glacite Armor can be salvaged for 2 [[Ice Essence]].

## Tips
- Glacite Armor is only useful in the early game before acquiring a set of [[Yog Armor]] or [[Sorrow Armor]]. Its Expert Miner full set bonus grants +120 [[Mining Speed]], which is less than a full set of [[Sorrow Armor]]; additionally, despite granting more [[Mining Speed]] than [[Yog Armor]], it grants significantly less [[Mining Fortune]].

Item ID: GLACITE_LANTERN
Tooltip: Mining Deployable; Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK Place an orb for 5m buffing up to 5; players within 30 blocks.; Affects all players inside a Glacite Mineshaft, regardless of distance.; Costs 50% of max mana.; Orb Buff: Glacite Lantern ▪ Grants +80 Mining Speed.; ▪ Grants +20 Mining Fortune.; ▪ Grants +10 Heat Resistance.; ▪ Grants +5 Cold Resistance.; Only one deployable buff applies.; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 8.; EPIC DEPLOYABLE
A Glacite Lantern is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Deployables|Deployable]].
## Obtaining
### Forge
A Glacite Lantern can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VIII (8)]]. It takes 12 hours to forge.
#### Forging Tree
- [[Glacite Lantern]] 1 Glacite Lantern
  - 1 [[Titanium Lantern]] 1 [[Mithril Lantern]] 1 [[Dwarven Lantern]] 1 [[Refined Mithril]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Mithril]] 16 [[Enchanted Emerald]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Emerald]] 2 [[Refined Titanium]] 32 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 5,120 (80 stacks) [[Titanium]] 1 [[Enchanted Emerald Block]] 160 [[Enchanted Emerald]] 25,600 [[Emerald]]
  - 4 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] 16 [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 1,280 (20 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 102,400 (1,600 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]] 16 [[Gemstone|Fine Aquamarine Gemstone]] 1,280 [[Gemstone|Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone]] 102,400 [[Gemstone|Rough Aquamarine Gemstone]] 16 [[Gemstone|Fine Citrine Gemstone]] 1,280 [[Gemstone|Flawed Citrine Gemstone]] 102,400 [[Gemstone|Rough Citrine Gemstone]] 16 [[Gemstone|Fine Peridot Gemstone]] 1,280 [[Gemstone|Flawed Peridot Gemstone]] 102,400 [[Gemstone|Rough Peridot Gemstone]] 1,024 (16 stacks) [[Enchanted Glacite]] 163,840 (2,560 stacks) [[Glacite]]
  - 2 [[Enchanted Emerald Block]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Emerald]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Emerald]]
## Usage
Using the ability of a Glacite Lantern costs 50% of player's max [[Mana]], this can be reduced down to 40% using [[Mana Disintegrator|Mana Disintegrators]]. Casting the ability grants up to 5 players in 30 block radius (or all players in the [[Glacite Mineshafts]]) the following stats:
- +80 [[Mining Speed]]
- +20 [[Mining Fortune]]
- +10 [[Heat Resistance]]
- +5 [[Cold Resistance]]
When used alongside multiple or different lantern deployables, only one deployable effect applies and only the one of higher rarity will apply its effects.

The Glacite-Plated Chisel is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Chisels|Chisel]].
## Obtaining
A Glacite-Plated Chisel can be forged at [[The Forge]] with 1 [[Reinforced Chisel]], 1 [[Mithril Plate]], 8 [[Glacite Amalgamation|Glacite Amalgamations]] and 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IX (9)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Glacite-Plated Chisel]] 1 Glacite-Plated Chisel
  - 1 [[Reinforced Chisel]] 1 [[Chisel]] 5 [[Fossil Essence]] 2 [[Refined Tungsten]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Tungsten]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Tungsten]] 2 [[Refined Umber]] 320 [[Enchanted Umber]] 51,200 [[Umber]] 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]] 3 [[Glacite Jewel]]
  - 8 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] 32 [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 204,800 (3,200 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]] 32 [[Gemstone|Fine Aquamarine Gemstone]] 2,560 [[Gemstone|Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone]] 204,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Aquamarine Gemstone]] 32 [[Gemstone|Fine Citrine Gemstone]] 2,560 [[Gemstone|Flawed Citrine Gemstone]] 204,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Citrine Gemstone]] 32 [[Gemstone|Fine Peridot Gemstone]] 2,560 [[Gemstone|Flawed Peridot Gemstone]] 204,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Peridot Gemstone]] 2,048 (32 stacks) [[Enchanted Glacite]] 327,680 (5,120 stacks) [[Glacite]]
  - 1 [[Mithril Plate]] 1 [[Refined Titanium]] 16 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 2,560 [[Titanium]] 5 [[Refined Mithril]] 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 128,000 (2,000 stacks) [[Mithril]] 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]] 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]]
  - 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]] 3 [[Glacite Jewel]]
## Usage
A Glacite-Plated Chisel can be used in the [[Fossil Excavator]] with [[Suspicious Scrap|Suspicious Scraps]] to find [[Fossils]] and other loot.
### Forging
The Glacite-Plated Chisel, along with a variety of other items, can be used to forge a [[Perfect Chisel]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain X (10)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.

### Gemstones
Applying [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] will give different perks. Each Gemstone type grants a different buff to the Chisel, and is not affected by rarity, unlike all other instances of Gemstone being applied:

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GLEAMING_CRYSTAL
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Lustrous reforge when combined with a Mining Tool.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Mining Speed  Mining Fortune; Lustrous Bonus Grants +25 Mining Spread while Mayor Cole's Mining Fiesta perk is active.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 24.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Gleaming Crystal is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Lustrous]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Fortune]].
## Obtaining
Gleaming Crystal can be forged in [[The Forge]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]]. It takes 8 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Gleaming Crystal]] 1 Gleaming Crystal
  - 32 [[Glossy Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Refined Mithril]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Mithril]]
  - 2 [[Refined Diamond]] 4 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 640 (10 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 102,400 (1,600 stacks) [[Diamond]]
Gleaming Crystal can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Gleaming Crystal can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Lustrous]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Mining Speed]], which scale with the Mining Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXIV (24)]]. A Mining Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Lustrous]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Lustrous Bonus, which grants 25 [[Mining Spread]] when [[Cole]]'s Mining Fiesta perk is active.[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Does this work with Diaz?; When this info is added, remove this.)

Tooltip: Ability: Life Tap LEFT CLICK Convert 15⸎ Soulflow to gain up to 600 Overflow.; Health Cost: 800; Cooldown: 1s; Ability: Extreme Measures RIGHT CLICK Heal for 1,000.; Vitality Cost: 45; ☠ Requires Enderman Slayer 5.; EPIC
The Gloomlock Grimoire is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item. It is unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer V (5)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer V (5) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x [[Book]] 8x [[Enchanted Quartz Block]] 16x [[Null Ovoid]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Gunpowder]]|Recipe: [[Null Ovoid]] x4 ×4 + [[Enchanted Gunpowder]] x64 ×2 + [[Enchanted Quartz Block]] x4 ×2 + [[Book]] → Gloomlock Grimoire|
## Usage
Item:
Gloomlock Grimoire
[Effects]
[Ability: Life Tap LEFT CLICK; Convert 15 Soulflow (Soulflow (Mechanic)) to gain up to 600 Overflow Mana (Mana).; Health Cost (Health): 800; Cooldown: 1s Ability: Extreme Measures RIGHT CLICK; Heal for 1,000 Health.; Vitality Cost (Vitality): 45]
The Gloomlock Grimoire has two abilities: Life Tap and Extreme Measures.
- Life Tap is activated on LEFT CLICK, converting 15 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] to 600 [[Mana|Overflow Mana]], at the cost of 800 [[Health]]. It has a cooldown of 1s.
- Extreme Measures is activated on RIGHT CLICK, healing the player for 1,000 [[Health]] at the cost of 45 [[Vitality]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Using Life Tap without enough Soulflow ([[Soulflow (Mechanic)]])|Not enough soulflow!|
|Using Life Tap while having 600 Overflow Mana ([[Mana]])|Already at soulflow cap for the Grimoire!|
|Using Life Tap while under 800 [[Health]]|☠ You died.|
## Tips
- Gives slightly more [[Health]] per [[Vitality]] than [[Florid Zombie Sword]], although it does not heal nearby players.

# Glossy Gemstone
Aliases: Glossies, Glossy, Glossy (disambiguation), Glossy Mineral, Glossy mineral
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Events, Mining Fiesta, Mining
[Glossy Gemstone]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Large Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); [[Events Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 200,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GLOSSY_GEMSTONE
Tooltip: Drops rarely from Gemstones while Mayor Cole's Mining Fiesta is active.; You can see thousands of glittering reflections as you stare into its intricate patterns. Maybe you should stop gazing and keep mining...; Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC
A Glossy Gemstone is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item obtained by mining [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] during the [[Mining Fiesta]] event.
## Obtaining
Glossy Gemstones have a chance to drop when mining [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] on Public Islands during a [[Mining Fiesta]], following the formula below and its additive multipliers: Chance=Breaking Power5,000×(1+∑Multipliers)
  Where ∑Multipliers is the sum of the multipliers.

## Usage
### Crafting
Glossy Gemstones can be used to craft a total of 5 items, requiring a total of [[Glossy Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) Glossy Gemstones.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Glossy Mineral Talisman]]|None|1x [[Mineral Talisman]] [[Glossy Gemstone]] 16x Glossy Gemstone|Recipe: Glossy Gemstone x2 ×8 + [[Mineral Talisman]] → [[Glossy Mineral Talisman]]|
|Glossy Mineral Helmet ([[Glossy Mineral Armor]])|None|1x Mineral Helmet ([[Mineral Armor]]) [[Glossy Gemstone]] 16x Glossy Gemstone|Recipe: Glossy Gemstone x2 ×8 + [[Mineral Armor]] → [[Glossy Mineral Armor]]|
|Glossy Mineral Chestplate ([[Glossy Mineral Armor]])|None|1x Mineral Chestplate ([[Mineral Armor]]) [[Glossy Gemstone]] 16x Glossy Gemstone|Recipe: Glossy Gemstone x2 ×8 + [[Mineral Armor]] → [[Glossy Mineral Armor]]|
|Glossy Mineral Leggings ([[Glossy Mineral Armor]])|None|1x Mineral Leggings ([[Mineral Armor]]) [[Glossy Gemstone]] 16x Glossy Gemstone|Recipe: Glossy Gemstone x2 ×8 + [[Mineral Armor]] → [[Glossy Mineral Armor]]|
|Glossy Mineral Boots ([[Glossy Mineral Armor]])|None|1x Mineral Boots ([[Mineral Armor]]) [[Glossy Gemstone]] 16x Glossy Gemstone|Recipe: Glossy Gemstone x2 ×8 + [[Mineral Armor]] → [[Glossy Mineral Armor]]|
### Forging
Glossy Gemstones can also be used to forge a total of 7 items, requiring a total of [[Glossy Gemstone]] 224 (3 stacks plus 32) Glossy Gemstones.
|Slot|Name|Requirements|Forging Tree|
|-|-|-|-|

## Trivia
- Glossy Gemstones can be obtained from Gemstone blocks broken through [[Gemstone Spread]] or [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickaxe Abilities]] such as [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickobulus]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 3, 2024|Added Glossy Gemstone.|
|Aug 20, 2024|Buffed drop chance from flat 1/725 to scaling based on the block's [[Breaking Power]].|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Glossy Gemstone as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
|Aug 27, 2026|Nerfed drop chances by 5x in the formula.|
|Sep 30, 2026|Improved itemlore of Glossy Gemstones by adding Drops rarely from Gemstones while Mayor Cole's Mining Fiesta is active.|
|Sep 30, 2026|Confirmed the nerf to drop chances by 5x.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Added Glossy Gemstone.|
|Aug 5, 2024|Glossy Gemstone becomes obtainable.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Changed drop chance from flat 1/725 to scaling based on the block's [[Breaking Power]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Glossy Gemstone as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Improved itemlore of Glossy Gemstones by adding Drops rarely from Gemstones while Mayor Cole's Mining Fiesta is active.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Nerfed drop chance of [[Glossy Gemstone]] Glossy Gemstones and Refined Minerals ([[Refined Mineral]]) from BP2,000 to BP10,000.|
|Oct 14, 2026|Buffed drop chance of [[Glossy Gemstone]] Glossy Gemstones and Refined Minerals ([[Refined Mineral]]) from BP10,000 to BP5,000.|
